%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000004445000160  
%            ALGOL ACCESS TO POSIX PROCEDURES                           0000004545000180  
%                                                                       0000004645000200  
% This ALGOL include file provides POSIX library procedure              0000004745000220  
% declarations for the POSIX MCPSUPPORT library interfaces.  This file  0000004845000240  
% can be included in ALGOL programs.  Programmers can access the new    0000004945000260  
% procedures using the names defined in this file instead of the actual 0000005045000280  
% names.  The names are chosen to mimic the "C" POSIX API calls.        0000005145000300  
% Additional symbols are defined to give more flexibility to the user   0000005245000320  
% for setting flags or values.                                          0000005345000340  
%                                                                       0000005445000360  
%  This file is organized in the following fashion:                     0000005545000380  
%                                                                       0000005645000400  
%  o  Part 1 of the file contains the interface declarations and        0000005745000420  
%     selector values                                                   0000005845000430  
%     1.   signal related calls                                         0000005945000440  
%     2.   semaphore related calls                                      0000006045000460  
%     3.   user/group info related calls                                0000006145000480  
%     4.   selector values are defined to symbols                       0000006245000490  
%                                                                       0000006345000500  
%  o  Part 2 of the file contains the macro definitions                 0000006445000520  
%     1.   errno values are defined to symbols                          0000006545000540  
%     2.   other flags are defined to symbols                           0000006645000580  
%                                                                       0000006745000600  
%  o  Part 3 of the file contains the re-definition of procedure        0000006845000620  
%     calls to simulate "C" interface call names.                       0000006945000640  
%                                                                       0000007045000660  
%  It is recommended that user access to these procedures be            0000007145000680  
%  made using the constants in PART 2 and the interfaces in PART 3.     0000007245000700  
%                                                                       0000007345000720  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000007445000740  
%                                                                       0000007545000760  
%                                                                       0000007645000780  
%  MCPSUPPORT Library Interfaces for POSIX                              0000007745000800  
%                                                                       0000007845000820  
%  General Definition Rules                                             0000007945000840  
%  ========================                                             0000008045000860  
%  1. All MCP interfaces which take pointers to character strings or    0000008145000880  
%     structures as input parameters will also take a by-value integer  0000008245000900  
%     string or structure length as a separate parameter.  The appli-   0000008345000920  
%     cation is responsible for providing this length. The MCP routines 0000008445000940  
%     will rely on this length information to determine how much of the 0000008545000960  
%     string or structure information is valid.                         0000008645000980  
%                                                                       0000008745001000  
%     In the case of strings, if the length is -1, the called function  0000008845001020  
%     will assume that the string is NULL terminated, and act according-0000008945001040  
%     ly.  If a string length is provided, it is assumed to NOT include 0000009045001060  
%     any trailing NULL.                                                0000009145001080  
%                                                                       0000009245001100  
%     All input structures with embedded strings will include an integer0000009345001120  
%     preceding the start of the string.  This integer will contain the 0000009445001140  
%     length of the string. If the length is -1, the MCP will assume    0000009545001160  
%     that the string is NULL terminated; if the length is not -1, it   0000009645001180  
%     will be taken as the length of the string,  not including the     0000009745001200  
%     NULL.                                                             0000009845001220  
%                                                                       0000009945001240  
%  2. All MCP interfaces which take pointers to character strings or    0000010045001260  
%     structures as output parameters will also return the length of the0000010145001280  
%     string or structure in a by-reference integer output parameter    0000010245001300  
%     unless such information can be returned as the interface result.  0000010345001320  
%     (See 5. for more details.)                                        0000010445001340  
%     Additionally, a by-value integer maximum string or structure      0000010545001360  
%     length input parameter must be provided.  The MCP interfaces      0000010645001380  
%     require a valid string or structure length maximum value, since   0000010745001400  
%     this length in conjunction with the actual size of the string or  0000010845001420  
%     structure container will determine how much of the string or      0000010945001440  
%     structure information will be written.                            0000011045001460  
%                                                                       0000011145001480  
%     The MCP interfaces will place a terminating NULL at the end of an 0000011245001500  
%     output character string if there is room and the returned length  0000011345001520  
%     will not include the NULL character.                              0000011445001540  
%                                                                       0000011545001560  
%     No error will be returned if there is no space for a NULL         0000011645001580  
%     character at the end of the data. However if there is insufficient0000011745001600  
%     room for the data, then EINVAL will be returned in the            0000011845001620  
%     by-reference integer output parameter ERRNO and unexpected result 0000011945001640  
%     will be returned. The documentation for each interface specifies  0000012045001660  
%     the behavior for all possible conditions.                         0000012145001700  
%                                                                       0000012245001720  
%     All output structures with embedded strings will include an       0000012345001740  
%     integer preceding the start of the string.  This integer will     0000012445001760  
%     contain the length of the string, excluding the NULL; overflow    0000012545001780  
%     conditions will be handled as described above.                    0000012645001800  
%                                                                       0000012745001820  
%  3. The application is responsible for guaranteeing the alignment of  0000012845001840  
%     embedded word numeric types (i.e.  integers) within structures for0000012945001860  
%     all MCP interfaces which take pointers to structures.  All        0000013045001880  
%     structures must start on a word boundary.                         0000013145001900  
%                                                                       0000013245001920  
%  4. For MCP interfaces with Selector parameters, any parameter (input 0000013345001940  
%     or output) is undefined if not used for that selector value. The  0000013445001960  
%     documentation for each interface states which parameters are      0000013545001980  
%     valid and which are undefined for each selector value.            0000013645002000  
%                                                                       0000013745002020  
%  5. All MCP interfaces return an integer or real result.  The result, 0000013845002040  
%     -1, indicates that an error has occurred and that ERRNO has       0000013945002060  
%     probably been modified.  A meaningful positive or negative result 0000014045002080  
%     may also be returned if specified by POSIX. MCP interfaces        0000014145002100  
%     never return a pointer result; however, if the POSIX              0000014245002120  
%     interface either returns a pointer, or an output pointer is its   0000014345002140  
%     only output parameter AND the function returns no other meaningful0000014445002160  
%     result, then the results of such functions will be the length of  0000014545002180  
%     the string supplied by the MCP.                                   0000014645002200  
%                                                                       0000014745002220  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000014845002240  
$$PAGE                                                                  0000014945003000  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                                   0000015045003020  
%    PART 1         %                                                   0000015145003040  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                                   0000015245003060

% POSIX_FCNTL CMD values    %                                           0000143545607460

% CMD values and associated parameters are as follows:                  0000143745607500  
   F_DUPFD  = 0 #, % Only supported for POSIX Global Files.  Causes file0000143845607520  
                   % descriptor to be copied into the first available   0000143945607540  
                   % file descriptor vector table entry.                0000144045607560  
   F_GETFD  = 1 #, % Returns current value of the file descriptor flags.0000144145607580  
   F_SETFD  = 2 #, % Sets the file descriptor flags.                    0000144245607600  
   F_GETFL  = 3 #, % Returns current value of the file status flags.    0000144345607620  
   F_SETFL  = 4 #, % Sets the file status flags.                        0000144445607640  
   F_GETLK  = 5 #, % Get the first lock which blocks the lock described 0000144545607660  
                   % by the flock structure.                            0000144645607680  
   F_SETLK  = 6 #, % An attempt is made to set or clear the lock        0000144745607700  
                   % specified by the flock structure.                  0000144845607720  
   F_SETLKW = 7 #; %Same as F_SETLK except when a blocking lock is found0000144945607740  
                                                                        0000145045607760  
DEFINE                                                                  0000145145607780  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%    0000145245607800  
%       POSIX flock STRUCTURE (POSIX_FCNTL)                        %    0000145345607820  
%..................................................................%    0000145445607840  
% The flock structure is used to specify locks for POSIX defined   %    0000145545607860  
% advisory locking.  Advisory locking is supported by POSIX_FCNTL. %    0000145645607880  
% POSIX_FCNTL uses FILELOCKER which also provides record locking   %    0000145745607900  
% for general A Series use.  The words in the flock structure map  %    0000145845607920  
% to FILELOCKER pararmeters.                                       %    0000145945607940  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%    0000146045607960  
    %-Word-offset-- %--Description--------------  %--FILELOCKER params  0000146145607980  
    L_TYPE   = 0 #, % Type of lock.               % TYPE                0000146245608000  
    L_WHENCE = 1 #, % Where L_START is based.     % WHENCE              0000146345608020  
    L_START  = 2 #, % Relative offset in bytes.   % START  RETURNSTART  0000146445608040  
    L_LEN    = 3 #, % Length of area in bytes.    % LEN    RETURNLEN    0000146545608060  
    L_PID    = 4 #, % Process ID of lock holder.  % n/a.   RETURNPID    0000146645608080  
    L_PAD1   = 5 #, % For future growth.          % n/a.                0000146745608100  
    FLOCK_LEN= 6 #; % Length of "flock" structure                       0000146845608120  
                                                                        0000146945608140  
   DEFINE                                                               0000147045608160  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000147145608180  
   % Type of lock (L_TYPE) %                                            0000147245608200  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000147345608220  
      F_RDLCK   =  1 #, % reader lock                                   0000147445608240  
      F_WRLCK   =  2 #, % writer lock                                   0000147545608260  
      F_UNLCK   =  3 #; % unlock (or not locked)                        0000147645608280  
                                                                        0000147745608300  
   DEFINE                                                               0000147845608320  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000147945608340  
   %      L_WHENCE         %                                            0000148045608360  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000148145608380  
      SEEK_SET  =  0 #, % apply offset from beginning of file.          0000148245608400  
      SEEK_CUR  =  1 #, % apply offset from current file pointer pos.   0000148345608420  
      SEEK_END  =  2 #; % apply offset from EOF (byte past last written)0000148445608440  
                                                                        0000148545608460  
   DEFINE                                                               0000148645608480  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000148745608500  
   % file descriptor flags %                                            0000148845608520  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000148945608540  
      FD_CLOEXECF = [0:1] #; % close the file descriptor upon execution 0000149045608560  
                             % of an exec-family functions.             0000149145608580  
                                                                        0000149245608600  
   DEFINE                                                               0000149345608620  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                          0000149445608640  
   % The file status flags and access method %                          0000149545608660

DEFINE    ACCESS(PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE,                   0000178046100010  
                       PATH_SEARCHRULE, AMODE, ERRNO)     =             0000178146100020

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000178446100050  
%   ACCESS: This procedure allows the caller to determine if the        0000178546100060  
%           permissions specified via AMODE are available for the file  0000178646100070  
%           specified by PATH.                                          0000178746100080  
%                                                                       0000178846100090  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000178946100100  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000179046100110  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000179146100120  
%           AMODE (integer) - Specifies Bitwise inclusive OR of the     0000179246100130  
%                 following set of permissions:                         0000179346100140  
%                                                                       0000179446100150  
%               NAME/VALUE   BIT   Permission                           0000179546100160  
%               ----------  -----  ----------                           0000179646100170  
%               R_OK = 4   [2:1]  Read                                  0000179746100180  
%               W_OK = 2   [1:1]  Write                                 0000179846100190  
%               X_OK = 1   [0:1]  Execute/Search                        0000179946100200  
%               F_OK = 0    ---   Existence (always checked)            0000180046100210  
%                                                                       0000180146100220  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000180246100230  
%                                                                       0000180346100240  
%   RESULT:                                                             0000180446100250  
%     If an error occurs, ERRNO is set and a -1 result is returned.     0000180546100260  
%     Otherwise 0 is returned indicating the file exists and the        0000180646100270  
%     caller has the access permissions specified by AMODE.             0000180746100280  
%     If the specified file name is shared by both a file and a         0000180846100290  
%     directory, access will be checked for the directory.              0000180946100300  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000181046100310  
                                                                        0000181146100320  
DEFINE    ALARM(SECS, ERRNO)     =                                      0000181246200000  
            POSIX_NANOALARM(SECS, ERRNO) #;                             0000181346200020  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000181446200040  
%   ALARM: causes the system to send the calling process a SIGALARM     0000181546200060  
%          signal after a specified number of seconds elapse.           0000181646200080  
%                                                                       0000181746200100  
%   INPUT: SECS (real) - number of elapsed seconds before the signal    0000181846200120  
%                        is released.                                   0000181946200140  
%                                                                       0000182046200160  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000182146200180  
%                                                                       0000182246200200  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns the number0000182346200220  
%           of seconds left in the previous alarm request. The procedure0000182446200240  
%           returns 0 if there was no previous alarm request.           0000182546200260  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO      0000182646200280  
%           is set to indicate the error.                               0000182746200300  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000182846200320  
                                                                        0000182946200340  
                                                                        0000183046200360  
DEFINE    CHDIR(PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN,                               0000183146202010  
                                PATH_TYPE, PATH_SEARCHRULE, ERRNO) =    0000183246202020  
POSIX_CHANGEDIR(PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN,                               0000183346202030  
                                PATH_TYPE, PATH_SEARCHRULE, ERRNO) #;   0000183446202040  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000183546202050  
%   CHDIR: changes the current working directory                        0000183646202060  
%                                                                       0000183746202070  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000183846202080  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000183946202090  
%           defines in this file. SEARCHRULE_POSIX is the only valid    0000184046202100  
%           option for CHDIR function.                                  0000184146202110  
%                                                                       0000184246202120  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000184346202130  
%                                                                       0000184446202140  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns 0.        0000184546202150  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000184646202160  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000184746202170  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000184846202180

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000191446216200  
%   EXECVE:                                                             0000191546216210  
%     This procedure handles all the user exec interfaces.              0000191646216220  
%                                                                       0000191746216230  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000191846216240  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000191946216250  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000192046216260  
%     ARGV - an ebcdic array containing parameters to be passed to the  0000192146216270  
%        program being execed.                                          0000192246216280  
%     ARGV_OFF (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000192346216290  
%     ARGV_LEN (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000192446216300  
%     ARGVINDX - an integer array of indexes into ARGV which point to   0000192546216310  
%        the start of each argument.                                    0000192646216320  
%     ARGVINDX_OFF (integer) - offset into ARGVINDX of where the        0000192746216330  
%        indexes start.                                                 0000192846216340  
%     ARGVINDX_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ARGV             0000192946216350  
%        (number of indexes).                                           0000193046216360  
%     ARGVSZ - an integer array containing to length of each parameter  0000193146216370  
%        in ARGV pointed to by the index found in ARGVINDX.             0000193246216380  
%     ARGVSZ_OFF (integer) - offset into ARGVSZ of where the sizes      0000193346216390  
%        are stored.                                                    0000193446216400  
%     ARGVSZ_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ARGV (number       0000193546216410  
%        of sizes). Must be equal to ARGVINDX_LEN.                      0000193646216420  
%     ENVP - an ebcdic array containing environment parameters to be    0000193746216430  
%        passed to the program being execed.                            0000193846216440  
%     ENVP_OFF (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000193946216450  
%     ENVP_LEN (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000194046216460  
%     ENVPINDX - an integer array of indexes into ENVP which point to   0000194146216470  
%        the start of each environment parameter.                       0000194246216480  
%     ENVPINDX_OFF (integer) - offset into ENVPINDX of where the indexes0000194346216490  
%        start.                                                         0000194446216500  
%     ENVPINDX_LEN (integer) - number of environment parameters in ENVP 0000194546216510  
%        (number of indexes).                                           0000194646216520  
%     ENVPSZ - an integer array containing to length of each environment0000194746216530  
%        parameter in ENVP pointed to by the index found in ENVPINDX.   0000194846216540

%        stored.                                                        0000195046216560  
%     ENVPSZ_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ENVP (number of    0000195146216570

%                                                                       0000195346216590  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000195446216600  
%                                                                       0000195546216610  
%   RETURN: If this procedure returns to the calling process, an error  0000195646216620

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000250046450080  
%   SEMCTL: provides a set of semaphore control operations as           0000250146450100  
%           specified by the CMD parameter.                             0000250246450120  
%                                                                       0000250346450140  
%   INPUT: SEMID (integer) - semaphore id for a set                     0000250446450160  
%          SEMNUM (integer) - semaphore member number within a set      0000250546450180  
%          CMD (integer) - action to be performed                       0000250646450200  
%              IPC_STAT    %read semaphore values of the semaphore set  0000250746450220  
%              IPC_SET     %set semaphore values of the semaphore set   0000250846450240  
%              IPC_RMID    %remove semaphore id from the system         0000250946450260  
%              SEM_GETNCNT %get # procs waiting for a certain sem value 0000251046450280  
%              SEM_GETPID  %get id of last process to operate on sem set0000251146450300  
%              SEM_GETVAL  %get the semaphore value                     0000251246450320  
%              SEM_GETALL  %get all semaphore values in the set         0000251346450340  
%              SEM_GETZCNT %get # procs waiting for sem value to be 0   0000251446450360  
%              SEM_SETVAL  %set a semaphore value                       0000251546450380  
%              SEM_SETALL  %set all semaphore value in the set          0000251646450400  
%          VAL (integer) - semaphore value for the SEM_SETVAL command   0000251746450420  
%          ARG (real array) - contains the following information:       0000251846450440  
%              If CMD = SEM_GETALL or SEM_SETALL, ARG contains every    0000251946450460  
%                 semvals in the set of semaphores.                     0000252046450480

%                 semid_ds structure. For more information about this   0000252246450520

%                 of this include file.                                 0000252446450560

DEFINE    SIGACTION(SIG, ACT_PROC, ACT, ACT_OFF, ACT_LEN,               0000282446660000

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000283046660120  
%   SIGACTION: allows the calling process to examine and/or specify     0000283146660140  
%              the action to be associated with a specific signal.      0000283246660160  
%                                                                       0000283346660180  
%   INPUT: SIG (integer) - signal number (refer to the sig values       0000283446660200  
%                          defined in this file)                        0000283546660220  
%          ACT_PROC - signal handling procedure to be invoked when a    0000283646660240  
%                     specified signal has been occurred. This procedure0000283746660260  
%                     must be declared as an integer procedure and take 0000283846660280  
%                     ten integer variables.                            0000283946660300  
%                                                                       0000284046660320  
%                INPUT:                                                 0000284146660340  
%                   INFO1 - version and signal number                   0000284246660360  
%                   INFO2 - sa_handler                                  0000284346660380  
%                   INFO3 - additional signal state information         0000284446660400  
%                   INFO4 - pid for si_pid member of siginfo structure  0000284546660420  
%                   INFO5 - uid for si_uid member of siginfo structure  0000284646660440  
%                   INFO6 - signal value for si_value member of the     0000284746660460  
%                           siginfo structure                           0000284846660480  
%                   INFO7 - for future expansion                        0000284946660500  
%                   INFO8 - for future expansion                        0000285046660520  
%                   INFO9 - for future expansion                        0000285146660540  
%                   INFO10 - for future expansion                       0000285246660560  
%                RETURN:                                                0000285346660580  
%                   result not used                                     0000285446660600  
%                                                                       0000285546660620  
%          ACT (real array) - the sigaction structure                   0000285646660640  
%              This structure contains the following 3 words:           0000285746660660  
%                sa_handler - SIG_FDFL, SIG_IGN or a unique number      0000285846660680  
%                             which is passed as the INFO2 parameter    0000285946660700  
%                             to the ACT_PROC procedure.                0000286046660720  
%                sa_mask    - a set of signals to be blocked during the 0000286146660740  
%                             execution of the signal-catching function 0000286246660760  
%                sa_flag    - flags which affect the behavior of signal 0000286346660780  
%                                                                       0000286446660800  
%          ACT_OFF (integer) - word offset into the array ACT           0000286546660820  
%                                                                       0000286646660840  
%          ACT_LEN (integer) -  the length of the sigaction structure   0000286746660860  
%              If the length is 0, then the ACT is a null structure.    0000286846660880  
%                                                                       0000286946660900  
%          OACT_OFF (integer) - word offset into the ACT structure      0000287046660920  
%          OACT_MAX (integer) - the length of the sigaction structure   0000287146660940  
%               If length is 0, then the ACT is a null structure.       0000287246660960  
%   OUTPUT:                                                             0000287346660980  
%          OACT (real array) - the sigaction structure                  0000287446661000  
%                                                                       0000287546661020  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns 0.        0000287646661040  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000287746661060

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000320046830060  
%   STAT: Returns the file status associated with the file named in     0000320146830070  
%          the PATH.                                                    0000320246830080  
%                                                                       0000320346830090  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000320446830100  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000320546830110  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000320646830120  
%           BUF_OFF (integer) - the word offset into the array BUF      0000320746830130  
%           BUF_MAX (integer) - max size in words starting from BUF_OFF 0000320846830140  
%                         to be filled in                               0000320946830150  
%                                                                       0000321046830160  
%   OUTPUT: BUF (real array) - holds the file status structure.         0000321146830170

%           file status structure in the array BUF.                     0000321646830220  
%           If BUF_MAX does not have enough space to hold the file      0000321746830230  
%           status structure, then it will be truncated.                0000321846830240  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000321946830250  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000322046830260  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000322146830270  
                                                                        0000322246830280  
INTEGER PROCEDURE STRERROR(BUFF, BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO);            0000322346850080  
VALUE                            BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO;             0000322446850100  
REFERENCE                  BUFF;                                        0000322546850120  
EBCDIC ARRAY               BUFF[0];                                     0000322646850140  
INTEGER                          BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO;             0000322746850160  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000322846850180  
%   STRERROR: converts an error number to a string and returns the      0000322946850200  
%             string in the return argument.                            0000323046850220  
%                                                                       0000323146850240  
%   INPUT: ERRNO (integer) - error number                               0000323246850260  
%                                                                       0000323346850280  
%   OUTPUT: BUFF (EBCDIC array) - place holder for an error message     0000323446850300  
%           BUFF_OFF (integer) - byte offset into the array BUFF        0000323546850320  
%           BUFF_MAX (integer) - maximum length of the array BUFF       0000323646850340  
%                                                                       0000323746850360  
%   RETURN: length of string or -1 if an error occurred.                0000323846850380  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000323946850400  
BEGIN                                                                   0000324046850420  
   EBCDIC ARRAY EA[0:POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE-1];                           0000324146850440  
   INTEGER L;                                                           0000324246850460  
                                                                        0000324346850480  
   C_STRERROR(ERRNO, EA[0], FALSE);                                     0000324446850500  
   IF ERRNO >=  0 THEN                                                  0000324546850520  
   BEGIN                                                                0000324646850540  
      SCAN EA[0] FOR L:POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE UNTIL = 0;                  0000324746850560  
      L:=POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE-L;                                        0000324846850580  
      REPLACE BUFF[BUFF_OFF] BY EA[0] FOR MIN(BUFF_MAX,L);              0000324946850600  
      STRERROR := MIN(BUFF_MAX, L);                                     0000325046850610  
   END ELSE BEGIN                                                       0000325146850620  
      STRERROR := -1;                                                   0000325246850630  
   END;                                                                 0000325346850640  
END OF STRERROR;                                                        0000325446850650  
                                                                        0000325546850660  
DEFINE SVFORK(ERRNO) =                                                  0000325646850670  
       POSIX_FORK(SVFORKV, ERRNO) #;                                    0000325746850680  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000325846850690  
%   FORK:                                                               0000325946850700  
%        This procedure creates a clone of the entire calling process   0000326046850710  
%        by executing the top frame only.  Use it if you are going to   0000326146850720  
%        call exec() right away.  The parent thread is suspended        0000326246850730  
%        until the child() does an exec() or _exit().  After an exec(), 0000326346850740  
%        but before any user code is executed, the child waits on an MCP0000326446850750  
%        event.  The parent thread must call svfork_release(<child pid>)0000326546850760  
%        to allow the child to continue.  This gives the parent time    0000326646850770  
%        to enter information about the child before the child can      0000326746850780  
%        access it or exit().                                           0000326846850790  
%                                                                       0000326946850800  
%   INPUT: none                                                         0000327046850810  
%                                                                       0000327146850820  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000327246850830  
%                                                                       0000327346850840  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns 0.        0000327446850850  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000327546850860  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000327646850870  
%           If the function succeeds, a new process will be created that0000327746850880  
%           will be returned a zero;  the calling process will be       0000327846850890  
%           returned the MIX number of the new process.                 0000327946850900  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000328046850910  
                                                                        0000328146850920  
DEFINE SVFORK_RELEASE(PID, ERRNO) =                                     0000328246850925  
       POSIX_SETIDS(RELEASEVFORK_SV, PID, 0, ERRNO) #;                  0000328346850930  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000328446850935  
%   FORK:                                                               0000328546850940  
%        Releases the child thread created by svfork().                 0000328646850945  
%                                                                       0000328746850950  
%   INPUT: PID (integer) - the child's process id that is suspended     0000328846850955  
%                          after a svfork()/exec().                     0000328946850956

  GRABALOG(LOG,ALOG,NIVLOG,FECDIA,BLOG,DES,NIVDES)=                     0000371100241002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000371200242002  
    IF NIVLOG = 0 THEN                                                  0000371300243002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000371400244002  
      REPLACE POINTER(BLOG) BY "HD",                                    0000371500245002  
                               FECDIA  FOR 8 DIGITS,                    0000371600246010  
                               0       FOR 8 DIGITS,                    0000371700247002  
                               0       FOR 2 DIGITS;                    0000371800248002  
    WRITE(LOG[0],75,BLOG);                                              0000371900249002  
      LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOG.LASTRECORD;                              0000372000250002  
    END;                                                                0000372100251002  
    REPLACE DFAUT(ALOG) BY (NIVLOG+1) FOR 8 DIGITS;                     0000372200252002  
    REPLACE DFAUTFAN(ALOG2) BY (NIVLOG+1) FOR 8 DIGITS;                 0000372300252102  
   IF IDFINDATADI(ALOG)  > 0 AND                                        0000372400253002  
     (IDFFORMATO(ALOG) = 0 OR                                           0000372500254002  
      IDFFORMATO(ALOG) = 1 OR                                           0000372600255002  
      IDFFORMATO(ALOG) = 2 OR                                           0000372700256002  
      IDFFORMATO(ALOG) = 3)  THEN                                       0000372800257002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000372900267002  
       IF NIVDES = 0 THEN                                               0000373000268002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000373100269002  
        REPLACE POINTER(BLOG) BY "HD",FECDIA FOR 8 DIGITS,              0000373200270010  
                                 40"0" FOR 530;                         0000373300270102  
          WRITE(DES[0],90,BLOG);                                        0000373400271002  
         LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DES.LASTRECORD;                           0000373500272002  
         REPLACE POINTER(BLOG) BY "HD",                                 0000373600275002  
                                  FECDIA FOR   8 DIGITS,                0000373700276010  
                                  NIVLOG FOR   8 DIGITS,                0000373800277002  
                                  1      FOR   2 DIGITS,                0000373900278002  
                                 40"0"  FOR 430 ;                       0000374000279010  
         WRITE(LOG[0],75,BLOG);                                         0000374100280002  
       END;                                                             0000374200281002  
       APUN_DESC:= 0;                                                   0000374300282002  
      P_ALOG := POINTER(ALOG)+975;                                      0000374400282102  
      SCAN P_ALOG         FOR LEN_REG:530 UNTIL NEQ 48"00";             0000374500283002  
      IF LEN_REG > 0 THEN                                               0000374600284002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000374700285002  
         REPLACE POINTER(BLOG,4) BY (NIVLOG +1) FOR 8 DIGITS,           0000374800286002  
                               POINTER(ALOG,4)+1950 FOR 1060,           0000374900286102  
                                            0 FOR   2 DIGITS,           0000375000287002  
                                            0 FOR   8 DIGITS,           0000375100288002  
                                            2 FOR   2 DIGITS;           0000375200290002  
         WRITE(DES[NIVDES+1],90,BLOG);                                  0000375300291002  
         NIVDES:= * + 1;                                                0000375400292002  
         APUN_DESC:= NIVDES;                                            0000375500293002  
      END;                                                              0000375600294002  
                                                                        0000375700295002  
      P_ALOG := POINTER(ALOG)+445;                                      0000375800296002  
      SCAN P_ALOG         FOR LEN_REG:530 UNTIL NEQ 48"00";             0000375900297002  
      IF  LEN_REG > 0 THEN                                              0000376000298002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000376100299002  
         REPLACE POINTER(BLOG,4) BY (NIVLOG +1) FOR 8 DIGITS,           0000376200300002  
                            POINTER(ALOG,4)+890 FOR 1060,               0000376300301002  
                                              0 FOR   2 DIGITS,         0000376400302002  
                            APUN_DESC           FOR   8 DIGITS,         0000376500303002  
                                            01  FOR   2 DIGITS;         0000376600304002  
                                                                        0000376700305002  
         WRITE(DES[NIVDES+1],90,BLOG);                                  0000376800306002  
         NIVDES:= * + 1;                                                0000376900307002  
         APUN_DESC := NIVDES;                                           0000377000307012  
      END;                                                              0000377100308002  
                                                                        0000377200315002  
      REPLACE POINTER(BLOG,4) BY DFFUNCION(ALOG) FOR 890,               0000377300316002  
                        APUN_DESC           FOR 8  DIGITS,              0000377400317002  
                         1                  FOR 2  DIGITS     %STA      0000377500319002  
                              ;                                         0000377600320002  
    END                                                                 0000377700321002  
    ELSE                                                                0000377800322002  
       REPLACE POINTER(BLOG,4) BY DFFUNCION(ALOG)   FOR 890             0000377900323002  
                              ,0                    FOR 8   DIGITS      0000378000324002  
                              ,1                    FOR 2   DIGITS;     0000378100326002

       END                                                              0000380900336102  
    ELSE                                                                0000381000337002

  BORRAXPROC(LOGO,ALOG,FECDIA)=                                         0000392100386002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000392200387002  
     NUM_REG:=0;                                                        0000392300388002  
     REG_LEIDOS:=-1;                                                    0000392400389002  
     CLOSE (LOGO,LOCK);                                                 0000392500390002  
     LOGO.BLOCKSIZE := 10800;                                           0000392600391002  
                                                                        0000392700392002  
     WHILE NOT READ(LOGO[REG_LEIDOS:= * + 1],75,ALOG) AND               0000392800393002  
            INTEGER(POINTER(ALOG,4),2) NEQ 99 DO                        0000392900394002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000393000395002  
       IF     IDFFUNCION(ALOG)  = 1                AND                  0000393100396002  
%             IDFSISTEMA(ALOG)  = IDFSISTEMA(ALOG) AND                  0000393200397002  
              IDFTIPPROC(ALOG)  = PROCAELIM        AND                  0000393300398002  
              IDFSTATUS (ALOG)  = 1               THEN                  0000393400399002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000393500400002  
            NUM_REG:= * + 1;                                            0000393600401002  
            REPLACE POINTER(ALOG,4)+ 899 BY 3 FOR 1 DIGITS;             0000393700402002  
            WRITE(LOGO[REG_LEIDOS],75,ALOG);                            0000393800403002  
       END;                                                             0000393900404002  
     END;                                                               0000394000405002  
     CLOSE (LOGO,LOCK);                                                 0000394100406002  
     LOGO.BLOCKSIZE := 150;                                             0000394200407002  
%    REPLACE   BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                                  0000394300408002  
%    REPLACE MSGLJ  BY "S151/L010/001:I>050110<";                       0000394400409002  
%    REPLACE TXTMSG BY "REG.ELIM.X PROC. ",                             0000394500410002  
%                      PROCAELIM  FOR * DIGITS, " CON FECHA ",          0000394600411002

     IF BLOCK_SIZE = 150 THEN                                           0000399700472102

     FILE_AMP.BLOCKSIZE := 10800 ;                                      0000399900473002  
     FOR R:= LEV_INI STEP 1 UNTIL (LEV_INI + ADD_REC) DO                0000400000474002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000400100475002  
         WRITE (FILE_AMP[R],REG_SIZE,ARR_VACIO);                        0000400200476002  
     END;                                                               0000400300477002  
     CLOSE(FILE_AMP,LOCK);                                              0000400400478002  
     FILE_AMP.BLOCKSIZE := BLOCK_SIZE;                                  0000400500479002  
     IF BLOCK_SIZE = 150 THEN                                           0000400600479102  
        FILE_AMP.SYNCHRONIZE := VALUE (OUT);                            0000400700479202  
  END#,                                                                 0000400800487002

     TIEMPO = 3#,                                                       0000469300874002  
     ACQUIREL(E,MIX_LOCK) =                                             0000469400875002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000469500876002

      WHILE RES_LOCK > 1 DO                                             0000469700881102  
      BEGIN                                                             0000469800881112  
         TOTAL := * + TIEMPO;                                           0000469900881122  
         IF TOTAL > 15 THEN                                             0000470000881132  
         BEGIN                                                          0000470100881142  
%           MIX_LOCK := LOCKSTATUS(E).[47:24];                          0000470200881152  
            ERRORMSG;                                                   0000470300881157  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY "Llevo "                    ,             0000470400881162  
                              TOTAL FOR 4 DIGITS          ,             0000470500881172  
                              " Seg en espera del Lock,"  ,             0000470600881182  
                              " La mezcla "               ,             0000470700881192  
                              MIX_LOCK FOR 4 DIGITS       ,             0000470800881202  
                              " Lo tiene tomado"          ;             0000470900881212  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000471000881222  
        END;                                                            0000471100881232  
        RES_LOCK := LOCK(E,TIEMPO);                                     0000471200881242  
      END;                                                              0000471300881252

     END#,                                                              0000471500882002

     DFVALIDA(ALOG,SISTEMA,PRODUCTO,GIND)=                              0000472600894002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000472700895002  
       RESULT:=0;                                                       0000472800896002  
                                                                        0000472900896052  
       IF IDFMONEDA(ALOG) > 0 AND IDFMONEDA(ALOG) < 100 THEN            0000473000896102  
       ELSE                                                             0000473100896152  
          BEGIN                                                         0000473200896202  
             REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                       0000473300896252  
                     "ENVIO DE MOVIMIENTOS, MONEDA NO VALIDA ",         0000473400896302  
                     "MONEDA : ", IDFMONEDA(ALOG) FOR 4 DIGITS ;        0000473500896352  
             I_TIPO := 3                                       ;        0000473600896402  
             L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO)                    ;        0000473700896452  
             RESULT := 14                                      ;        0000473800896502  
             GRABAMOV := FALSE                                 ;        0000473900896552  
          END;                                                          0000474000896602  
                                                                        0000474100896652  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000474200896702  
          IF IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,1) = 0 THEN                                0000474300897002  
             BEGIN                                                      0000474400898002  
                REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                    0000474500898102  
                "ENVIO DE MOVIMIENTOS, CLAVE DE TRANSACCION ",          0000474600898122  
                "NO CATALOGADA ",                                       0000474700898202  
                "CVETRAN : ", NUM_CLAVE FOR 4 DIGITS,                   0000474800898302  
                ", INDICE  1" ;                                         0000474900898402  
                I_TIPO := 3;                                            0000475000898502  
%         L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                               0000475100898602  
                ERRORMSG;                                               0000475200899002  
                RESULT := 3;                                            0000475300903002  
             END;                                                       0000475400904002  
                                                                        0000475500904102  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000475600905002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000475700906002  
          GIND:= 0;                                                     0000475800907002  
%         IND_CAT :=0;                                                  0000475900908002  
          WHILE GIND:= * + 1          LEQ 5 AND                         0000476000909002  
                IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND)   > 0 DO                          0000476100910002  
            BEGIN                                                       0000476200911002  
              NUM_CLAVE := IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND);                       0000476300911302  
              IF IADFCVECOL(NUM_CLAVE) LEQ 0 THEN                       0000476400911402  
              BEGIN                                                     0000476500911452  
                 ERRORMSG;                                              0000476600911502  
                 REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                   0000476700914102  
                         "ENVIO DE MOVIMIENTOS, ",                      0000476800914122  
                         "CLAVE DE TRANSACCION NO CATALOGADA ",         0000476900914202  
                         "CVETRAN : ", NUM_CLAVE FOR 5 DIGITS,          0000477000914302  
                         ", INDICE ",GIND FOR 2 DIGITS;                 0000477100914402  
                 I_TIPO := 3;                                           0000477200914502  
%                L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                        0000477300914602  
                 RESULT := 3;                                           0000477400920002  
              END;                                                      0000477500921102  
            END;                                                        0000477600922124  
       END;                                                             0000477700923002  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000477800924002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000477900925002  
          GIND:= 0;                                                     0000478000926002  
          WHILE GIND:= * + 1          LEQ 5 AND                         0000478100927002  
                IDFIMPOCUR(ALOG,GIND)   > 0 DO                          0000478200928002  
            BEGIN                                                       0000478300929002  
                NUM_CLAVE := IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND);                     0000478400929702  
                                                                        0000478500931002  
                IF (IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 2  OR                     0000478600932002  
                    IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 3) AND                    0000478700933002  
                    IDFSUCPROM(ALOG)            LEQ  0  THEN            0000478800934002  
                BEGIN                                                   0000478900934052  
                    REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                0000479000934102  
                            "ENVIO DE MOVIMIENTOS, ",                   0000479100934202  
                            "SUCURSAL PROMOTORA ERRONEA ",              0000479200934302  
                            "SUC. PROM. : ", IDFSUCPROM(ALOG) FOR       0000479300934342  
                            4 DIGITS ;                                  0000479400934382  
                    I_TIPO := 3;                                        0000479500934402  
%                   L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                     0000479600934502  
                    RESULT := 5;                                        0000479700935002  
                END;                                                    0000479800935102  
                                                                        0000479900935202  
                IF (IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 1  OR                     0000480000936002  
                    IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 3) AND                    0000480100937002  
                    IDFSUCINI(ALOG)              LEQ 0  THEN            0000480200938002  
                BEGIN                                                   0000480300938052  
                    REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                0000480400938102  
                            "ENVIO DE MOVIMIENTOS, ",                   0000480500938202  
                            "SUCURSAL INICIATIVA ERRONEA ",             0000480600938302  
                            "SUC. INIC. : ", IDFSUCINI(ALOG) FOR        0000480700938342  
                            4 DIGITS ;                                  0000480800938382  
                    I_TIPO := 3;                                        0000480900938402  
%                   L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                     0000481000938502  
                    RESULT := 6;                                        0000481100939002  
                END;                                                    0000481200939102  
            END;                                                        0000481300941134  
       END;                                                             0000481400942002  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000481500943002  
       IF IDFORIGEN(ALOG) = 1 OR                                        0000481600944002  
          IDFORIGEN(ALOG) = 2 OR                                        0000481700945002  
          IDFORIGEN(ALOG) = 3 THEN                                      0000481800946002  
       ELSE                                                             0000481900947002  
          BEGIN                                                         0000482000947052  
             REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                       0000482100947102  
                     "ENVIO DE MOVIMIENTOS, ",                          0000482200947122  
                     "ORIGEN NO VALIDO DIFERENTE DE 1, 2 ¿ 3 ",         0000482300947202  
                     "ORIGEN : ", IDFORIGEN(ALOG) FOR 2 DIGITS ;        0000482400947222  
             I_TIPO := 3;                                               0000482500947302  
%            L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                            0000482600947402  
             RESULT:= 10;                                               0000482700948002  
          END;                                                          0000482800948052  
     END#,                                                              0000482900949002

 INTEGER PROCEDURE CALINDSIS(SISTEMA,PRODUCTO);                         0000519301286002  
 VALUE   SISTEMA,PRODUCTO;                                              0000519401287002  
 INTEGER SISTEMA,PRODUCTO;                                              0000519501288002  
%***********************************                                    0000519601289002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000519701290002  
   CASE SISTEMA OF                                                      0000519801291002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000519901292002  
     500:CASE PRODUCTO OF                                               0000520001293002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000520101294002  
            066:CALINDSIS:=0;                                           0000520201295002  
            500:CALINDSIS:=1;                                           0000520301296002  
            001:CALINDSIS:=2;                                           0000520401297002  
           ELSE:CALINDSIS:=99;                                          0000520501298002  
         END;                                                           0000520601299002  
     084:CASE PRODUCTO OF                                               0000520701300002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000520801301002  
            084:CALINDSIS:=1;                                           0000520901302002  
           ELSE:CALINDSIS:=99;                                          0000521001303002  
         END;                                                           0000521101304002  
     087:CASE PRODUCTO OF                                               0000521201305002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000521301306002  
            087:CALINDSIS:=1;                                           0000521401307002  
           ELSE:CALINDSIS:=99;                                          0000521501308002  
         END;                                                           0000521601309002  
     408:CALINDSIS:=1;                                                  0000521701310002  
     151:IF PRODUCTO = 1151 THEN                                        0000521801311002  
            CALINDSIS:=1                                                0000521901312002  
         ELSE                                                           0000522001313002  
            CALINDSIS:=99;                                              0000522101314002  
     701:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000522201315002  
     703:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000522301316002  
     403:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000522401317002  
     404:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000522501318002  
     264:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000522601319002  
     333:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000522701320002  
     ELSE:CALINDSIS:=99;                                                0000522801321002  
   END;                                                                 0000522901322002  
 END OF CALINDSIS;                                                      0000523001323002

%*******************************************************************    0000523901332002

%*******************************************************************    0000530101360002  
 BOOLEAN PROCEDURE EXTERNO_P025;                                        0000530201361002  
%*******************************************************************    0000530301362002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000530401363002  
    STAT_P025.STATUS := VALUE(NEVERUSED);                               0000530501364002  
    REPLACE CTLVER_ID BY "S151P025ACTS";                                0000530601365002  
    IF DAME_TIT(CTLVER_ID,CTLVER_NOM) < 0  THEN                         0000530701366002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000530801367002  
       REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                            0000530901368002  
       REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG,"I>020901< LIBRARY REGISTRA, ",          0000531001369002  
               "ERROR EN CTLVERS, NO EXISTE IDENTIFICADOR DE ",         0000531101370002  
               "P025 ASINCRONO S151 (S151P025ACT)" ;                    0000531201371002  
       LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                 0000531301372002  
     REPLACE NOMP025 BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDSDOMOV/02MTP   ";  0000531401373002  
    END                                                                 0000531501374002  
    ELSE                                                                0000531601375002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000531701376002  
       REPLACE NOMP025 BY CTLVER_NOM UNTIL = " ", " ";                  0000531801377002  
       POSICION := SIZE(NOMP025);                                       0000531901378002  
       IF POSICION > 16 THEN                                            0000532001379002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000532101380002  
          POINT_P025 := NOMP025[POSICION - 16];                         0000532201381002  
          REPLACE MTP_P025 BY POINT_P025 FOR 8;                         0000532301382002  
       END                                                              0000532401383002  
       ELSE                                                             0000532501384002  
          REPLACE MTP_P025 BY "02MTP   ";                               0000532601385002  
    END;                                                                0000532701386002  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                                  0000532801387002  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY "COPY ", NOMP025 UNTIL = " " ,                      0000532901388002  
                    " AS ",                                             0000533001389002  
                    "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDS",                    0000533101390002  
                      SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,                   0000533201391002  
                      "/",                                              0000533301392002  
                      MTP_P025 FOR 8,                                   0000533401393002  
                      " FROM CMEMP(PACK) TO CMEMP(PACK)",               0000533501394002  
                      48"00";                                           0000533601395002  
    DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                        0000533701396002  
    REPLACE NOM_ASINC BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDS",               0000533801397002  
                          SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,               0000533901398002  
                          "/",                                          0000534001399002  
                          MTP_P025 FOR 8,                               0000534101400002  
                          " ON CMEMP.",                                 0000534201401002  
                          48"00";                                       0000534301402002  
    REPLACE STAT_P025.NAME BY NOM_ASINC;                                0000534401404002  
    REPLACE PARAM BY SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS ;                   0000534501405002  
    PROCESS EJECUTA(PARAM)[STAT_P025];                                  0000534601406002  
    WAIT((3));                                                          0000534701407002  
 END OF EXTERNO_P025;                                                   0000534801408002

 PROCEDURE CARGAMOV;                                                    0000548201417002

 BEGIN                                                                  0000548501420002  
   HEX ARRAY HALOG_CBII[0] = ALOG1;                                     0000548601420102  
%  INTEGER RESULT,INDSIS, APUN_DESC,IND_CAT,NUM_CLAVE,NUM_REG,          0000548701421002  
   INTEGER RESULT,NUM_REG,APUN_DESC,NUM_CLAVE,                          0000548801421102  
           I_I, RESULT_CTL, ICLAVTRAN, ITIPOMOVI,                       0000548901422002

%----------------------                                                 0000550101430002  
 PROCEDURE CARGAMEMORY;                                                 0000550201431002  
%----------------------                                                 0000550301432002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000550401433002  
   IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) = 1 THEN                                        0000550501434002  
      DFVALIDA(ALOG1,IDFSISTEMA(ALOG1),IDFPRODUCT(ALOG1),GIND1);        0000550601435002  
   IF GRABAMOV THEN                                                     0000550701436002  
   GRABALOG(LOGS[NUMDIAC1],ALOG1,NIVLOG_A[NUMDIAC1],FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1],  0000550801437002  
                              BLOG1,DESS[NUMDIAC1],NIVDES_A[NUMDIAC1]); 0000550901438002  
                                                                        0000551001439002  
   IF LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1] - NIVLOG_A[NUMDIAC1] < 5 THEN                 0000551101440002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000551201441002  
      REGSIZE := 75;                                                    0000551301441102  
      AMPL_REG := 20000;                                                0000551401441202  
      DFAMPLIAFILE(LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1],AMPL_REG,A_VACIO, 0000551501442002  
                   REGSIZE);                                            0000551601442102  
      READ(LOGS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                          0000551701443002  
      LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOGS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                   0000551801444002  
   END;                                                                 0000551901445002  
                                                                        0000552001446002  
                                                                        0000552101447002  
   IF LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1] - NIVDES_A[NUMDIAC1] < 5 THEN                 0000552201448002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000552301449002  
      REGSIZE:=90;                                                      0000552401449102  
      AMPL_REG := 5000;                                                 0000552501449202  
      DFAMPLIAFILE(DESS[NUMDIAC1],LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1],AMPL_REG,A_VACIO, 0000552601450002  
                   REGSIZE);                                            0000552701450102  
      READ(DESS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                          0000552801451002  
      LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DESS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                   0000552901452002  
   END;                                                                 0000553001453002  
                                                                        0000553101454002  
 END OF CARGAMEMORY;                                                    0000553201455002
