%    SIZESDOS1:= 216;                                                   0000599401783015  
% REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1) BY 48"00" FOR 216;                             0000599501783020  
 IF SIZESDOS1 > 390 THEN                                                0000599601783220  
    SIZESDOS1:= 390;                                                    0000599701783420  
 REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1) BY 48"00" FOR 390;                              0000599801783620  
%INICIO CJMG CAMBIO PARA PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                           0000599901783820  
 REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1) BY DATOS FOR SIZESDOS1;                         0000600001784002  
 J_J    := -1;                                                          0000600101785002  
 BOOL_J := FALSE ;                                                      0000600201786002  
 ISDOFUNC :=  IDFSDOFUNC(ASDO1) ;                                       0000600301786012  
 ISDOSIST :=  IDFSDOSIST(ASDO1) ;                                       0000600401786022  
 ISDOPROD :=  IDFSDOPROD(ASDO1) ;                                       0000600501786032  
 ISDOINST :=  IDFSDOINST(ASDO1) ;                                       0000600601786042  
%ISDOCONTR:=  IDFSDOCONTR(ASDO1);                                       0000600701786052  
%ISDOEDCTA:=  IDFSDOEDCTA(ASDO1);                                       0000600801786062  
 ISDOFECPR:=  IDFSDOFECPR(ASDO1);                                       0000600901786072  
                                                                        0000601001786082  
 WHILE J_J:= * + 1 LEQ 9 AND NOT BOOL_J DO                              0000601101787002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000601201788002  
    IF IDFSDOFECPR(ASDO1) = FEC_DIA[J_J] THEN                           0000601301789002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000601401790002  
       NUM_FEC  := J_J ;                                                0000601501791002  
       BOOL_J   := TRUE;                                                0000601601792002  
    END;                                                                0000601701793002  
 END;                                                                   0000601801794002  
 IF NOT BOOL_J THEN                                                     0000601901795002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000602001795102  
       REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                             0000602101795202  
               "FECHA CONTABLE NO VALIDA EN SALDOS";                    0000602201795302  
       I_TIPO := 3;                                                     0000602301795402  
%      L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                                  0000602401795502  
       RESULT_SDO := 4;                                                 0000602501796002  
    END                                                                 0000602601796102  
 ELSE                                                                   0000602701797002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000602801798002  
    CASE IDFSDOFUNC(ASDO1) OF                                           0000602901799002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000603001800002  
       80:BEGIN                                                         0000603101801002  
             NIVSDO_A[NUM_FEC] := 0   ;                                 0000603201802002  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC]);                                     0000603301803002  
             SDOS[NUM_FEC].MYUSE:= 1   ;                                0000603401804002  
%INICIO CJMG CAMBIO PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                                0000603501804052  
%             SDOS[NUM_FEC].BLOCKSIZE := 10800;                         0000603601804102  
%FIN CJMG CAMBIO PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                                   0000603701804602  
             CARGAMEMSDO;                                               0000603801805002  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC],LOCK);                                0000603901806002  
             SDOS[NUM_FEC].MYUSE:= 3   ;                                0000604001807002  
          END;                                                          0000604101808002  
       81:CARGAMEMSDO;                                                  0000604201809002  
       82:BEGIN                                                         0000604301810002  
             CARGAMEMSDO;                                               0000604401811002  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC],LOCK);                                0000604501812002  
          END;                                                          0000604601813002  
       ELSE:                                                            0000604701814002  
            BEGIN                                                       0000604801814102  
               REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                     0000604901814202  
                       "FUNCION NO VALIDA EN SALDOS";                   0000605001814302  
               I_TIPO := 3;                                             0000605101814402  
%              L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                          0000605201814502  
               RESULT_SDO:= 2;                                          0000605301814602  
            END;                                                        0000605401814702  
    END;                                                                0000605501815002  
 END;                                                                   0000605601816002  
 IF RESULT_SDO > 0 THEN                                                 0000605701817002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000605801818002  
       REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4)+1078 BY RESULT_SDO FOR 2 DIGITS;        0000605901826002  
       WRITE(ERRORES1[NIVERROR1],90,ASDO1);                             0000606001827002  
       NIVERROR1 := * + 1;                                              0000606101828002  
    END;                                                                0000606201829002  
 REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(ASDO1) FOR SIZESDOS1;                         0000606301830002  
 CARGASALDOS:= RESULT_SDO;                                              0000606401831002  
                                                                        0000606501831102  
   IF RESULT_SDO > 0 THEN                                               0000606601831202  
      IF NUM_ERROR[RESULT_SDO] > 0 THEN                                 0000606701831212  
      BEGIN                                                             0000606801831222  
         NUM_ERROR[RESULT_SDO] := * + 1;                                0000606901831232  
         IF (NUM_ERROR[RESULT_SDO] - ((NUM_ERROR[RESULT_SDO] DIV 100 )  0000607001831242  
                                   * 100)) = 0 THEN                     0000607101831262  
            BEGIN                                                       0000607201831282  
               REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                     0000607301831292  
                    "LLEVO ", NUM_ERROR[RESULT_SDO] FOR 8 DIGITS,       0000607401831302  
                    ", MENSAJES DE ERROR DEL TIPO DE RESULT SDO = ",    0000607501831312  
                       RESULT_SDO FOR 2 DIGITS;                         0000607601831322  
               I_TIPO := 2;                                             0000607701831332  
               L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                          0000607801831342  
            END;                                                        0000607901831352  
      END                                                               0000608001831362  
      ELSE                                                              0000608101831372  
         BEGIN                                                          0000608201831382  
            L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                             0000608301831392  
            NUM_ERROR[RESULT_SDO] := 1;                                 0000608401831402  
         END;                                                           0000608501831412  
                                                                        0000608601831422  
 END CARGASDO;                                                          0000608701832002

 INTEGER PROCEDURE CONTROLES(DATOS);                                    0000609801842002

 BEGIN                                                                  0000610001844002  
%*************************************************************          0000610101845002  
  DEFINE                                                                0000610201846002  
                                                                        0000610301847002  
        FUNCION       = INTEGER(DATOS[0],4)   #,                        0000610401848002  
        NUMLOG        = INTEGER(DATOS[4],2)   #;                        0000610501850002  
  INTEGER RESULT,I,J,K, TIME_FECHA;                                     0000610601851002

%----------------------                                                 0000610801855002  
% CONTROLES OUTER BLOCK                                                 0000610901856002  
%----------------------                                                 0000611001857002  
% ACQUIREL(E3,A3);                                                      0000611101858002  
  RESULT:=0;                                                            0000611201859002  
  CASE FUNCION OF                                                       0000611301860002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000611401861002  
     1:                                                                 0000611501862002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000611601863002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ)  ;                        0000611701864002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY "S151/L002/024:I>050110<";                    0000611801865002

                           "** FECHA DE CONSULTA: ",                    0000612001867002  
                           FEC_CON FOR 8 DIGITS," **";                  0000612101868010  
         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000612201869002  
         K:=-1;                                                         0000612301870002  
         WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                       0000612401871002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000612501872002  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** FECHA ", K+1  FOR * DIGITS, " ",      0000612601873002  
                                  FEC_DIA[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS,             0000612701874010  
                    ", NIVEL = ", NIVLOG_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS, " **";     0000612801875002  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000612901876002  
         END;                                                           0000613001877002  
         RESULT:=0                                                      0000613101878002  
       END;                                                             0000613201879002  
                                                                        0000613301880002  
     2:                                                                 0000613401881002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000613501882002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ)  ;                        0000613601883002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY "S151/L002/024:I>050110<";                    0000613701884002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** NIVELES DE ACTUALIZACION EN FILE **",    0000613801885002  
                           "** DESCRIPCIONES    : ";                    0000613901886002  
         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000614001887002  
         K:=-1;                                                         0000614101888002  
         WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                       0000614201889002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000614301890002  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** FECHA ", K+1  FOR * DIGITS, " ",      0000614401891002  
                                  FEC_DIA[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS,             0000614501892010  
                    ", NIVEL = ", NIVDES_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS, " **";     0000614601893002  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000614701894002  
         END;                                                           0000614801895002  
         RESULT:=0                                                      0000614901896002  
       END;                                                             0000615001897002  
     3:                                                                 0000615101898002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000615201899002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ)  ;                        0000615301900002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY "S151/L002/024:I>050110<";                    0000615401901002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** NIVELES DE ACT. EN S151 ",               0000615501902002  
                           "FECHA CONS : ",                             0000615601903002

         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000615801905002

         WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                       0000616001907002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000616101908002  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY " FECHA ", K+1  FOR * DIGITS, " ",        0000616201909002  
                                  FEC_DIA[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS,             0000616301910010  
                    ", NIVEL = ", NIVLOG_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS;            0000616401911002  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000616501912002  
            IF FEC_DIA[K] = FEC_CON THEN                                0000616601913002  
               IF STATUS_MIX_P015(K) > 0 THEN                           0000616701914002  
               BEGIN                                                    0000616801915002  
                  REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                    0000616901916002  
                  REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P015(K) FOR 7 DIGITS,         0000617001917002  
                                  " HI 20 ",                            0000617101918002  
                                    48"00";                             0000617201919002  
                  DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                          0000617301920002  
               END;                                                     0000617401921002  
         END;                                                           0000617501922002  
         RESULT:=0                                                      0000617601923002  
       END;                                                             0000617701924002  
    5 : BEGIN                                                           0000617801924052  
           REPLACE DATOS[4] BY 0 FOR 18 DIGITS;                         0000617901924102  
           K := -1;                                                     0000618001924152  
           WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                     0000618101924202  
              IF FEC_CON = FEC_DIA[K] THEN                              0000618201924252  
              BEGIN                                                     0000618301924302  
                 REPLACE DATOS[4] BY  K + 1        FOR 2 DIGITS,        0000618401924352  
                                      FEC_CON      FOR 8 DIGITS,        0000618501924402  
                                      NIVLOG_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS;        0000618601924452  
                 K:= 10;                                                0000618701924502  
              END;                                                      0000618801924552  
        END;                                                            0000618901924602  
                                                                        0000619001924652  
    10:CIERRALOG(0);                                                    0000619101925002  
    11:CIERRALOG(1);                                                    0000619201926002  
    12:CIERRALOG(2);                                                    0000619301927002  
    13:CIERRALOG(3);                                                    0000619401928002  
    14:CIERRALOG(4);                                                    0000619501929002

    18:CIERRALOG(8);                                                    0000619901933002  
    19:CIERRALOG(9);                                                    0000620001934002  
    20:BEGIN                                                            0000620101934402  
          IF B_INICIO THEN                                              0000620201934442  
             BEGIN                                                      0000620301934445  
                B_INICIO:=FALSE;                                        0000620401934452  
                WHILE RES_LINK := DELINKLIBRARY(LIB_OUTBOARD) < 0 DO ;  0000620501934457  
             END;                                                       0000620601934467  
       END;                                                             0000620701934477  
    21:BEGIN                                                            0000620801934802  
          IF NOT B_INICIO THEN                                          0000620901934822  
             BEGIN                                                      0000621001934842  
                B_INICIO:=TRUE;                                         0000621101934862  
                REPLACE LIB_OUTBOARD.FUNCTIONNAME BY "OUTBOARD." FOR 9; 0000621201934887  
                WHILE RES_LINK := LINKLIBRARY(LIB_OUTBOARD) < 0 DO  ;   0000621301934892  
             END;                                                       0000621401934902  
       END;                                                             0000621501934922  
    22:BEGIN                                                            0000621601935002  
          L:= -1;                                                       0000621701936002  
          WHILE L:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                         0000621801937002  
          BEGIN                                                         0000621901938002  
             CIERRALOG(L);                                              0000622001939002  
             IF STATUS_MIX_P015(L) > 0 THEN                             0000622101940002  
             BEGIN                                                      0000622201941002  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                      0000622301942002  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P015(L) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",  0000622401943002  
                                  48"00";                               0000622501944002  
                DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                            0000622601945002  
             END;                                                       0000622701946002  
             IF STATUS_MIX_P016(L) > 0 THEN                             0000622801946102  
             BEGIN                                                      0000622901946202  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                      0000623001946302  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P016(L) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",  0000623101946402  
                                  48"00";                               0000623201946502  
                DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                            0000623301946602  
             END;                                                       0000623401946702  
          END;                                                          0000623501947002  
          CLOSE (ERRORES1,LOCK);                                        0000623601948002  
%         NIVERROR1 := 0;                                               0000623701948102  
          WAIT((10));                                                   0000623801949002  
          LEVANTA_P015 := FALSE;                                        0000623901950002  
          LEVANTA_P016 := FALSE;                                        0000624001950102  
       END;                                                             0000624101951002  
                                                                        0000624201952002  
    23: BEGIN                                                           0000624301953002  
        LEVANTA_P015 := TRUE;                                           0000624401953102  
        LEVANTA_P016 := TRUE;                                           0000624501953202  
        END;                                                            0000624601953302  
    25:BEGIN                                                            0000624701954002  
          CPID_CARGAMOV := MYSELF.MIXNUMBER;                            0000624801954102  
          ACQUIREL(CAMBIA_FECHA,CPID_CARGAMOV);                         0000624901959002  
          L:= -1;                                                       0000625001960002  
          WHILE L:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                         0000625101961002  
          BEGIN                                                         0000625201962002  
             CIERRALOG(L);                                              0000625301963002  
             IF STATUS_MIX_P015(L) > 0 THEN                             0000625401964002  
             BEGIN                                                      0000625501965002  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                      0000625601966002  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P015(L) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",  0000625701967002  
                                  48"00";                               0000625801968002  
                DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                            0000625901969002  
             END;                                                       0000626001970002  
             IF STATUS_MIX_P016(L) > 0 THEN                             0000626101970012  
             BEGIN                                                      0000626201970022  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                      0000626301970032

%         IF STATUS_MIX_P025 > 0 THEN                                   0000626901972002  
%         BEGIN                                                         0000627001973002  
%            REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                         0000627101974002

          WAIT((10));                                                   0000627801980002  
          B_INICIO:=FALSE;                                              0000627901981002  
                                                                        0000628001982002  
          LEVANTA;                                                      0000628101983002  
          TIME_FECHA := 5;                                              0000628201984002  
          RELINQUISHL(CAMBIA_FECHA);                                    0000628301985002  
                                                                        0000628401986002  
       END;                                                             0000628501987002  
    30:BEGIN                                                            0000628601990002  
          VERSION_P015;                                                 0000628701991002  
          VERSION_P016;                                                 0000628801992002  
                                                                        0000628902004502  
      REPLACE CTLVER_ID BY "S151P025ACTS";                              0000629002005002  
      IF DAME_TIT(CTLVER_ID,CTLVER_NOM) < 0  THEN                       0000629102006002

                 "ERROR EN CTLVERS, NO EXISTE IDENTIFICADOR DE ",       0000629502010002

      END                                                               0000629902014002  
      ELSE                                                              0000630002015002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000630102016002  
         REPLACE NOMP025 BY CTLVER_NOM UNTIL = " ", " ";                0000630202017002

         BEGIN                                                          0000630502020002

            REPLACE MTP_P025 BY POINT_P025 FOR 8;                       0000630702022002

         ELSE                                                           0000630902024002

                             SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,            0000631202027002  
                             "/",                                       0000631302028002  
                             MTP_P025 FOR 10;                           0000631402029002  
      END;                                                              0000631502030002  
    END;                                                                0000631602031002  
    50: ACQUIREL(CAMBIA_FECHA,MYSELF.MIXNUMBER);                        0000631702032002  
    51: RELINQUISHL(CAMBIA_FECHA);                                      0000631802033002  
    52: ACQUIREL(E1,MYSELF.MIXNUMBER);                                  0000631902034002  
    53: ACQUIREL(E2,MYSELF.MIXNUMBER);                                  0000632002035002

       BEGIN                                                            0000632502070002  
          1:CLOSE(DES1,LOCK);                                           0000632602071002  
          2:CLOSE(DES2,LOCK);                                           0000632702072002

          4:CLOSE(DES4,LOCK);                                           0000632902074002  
          5:CLOSE(DES5,LOCK);                                           0000633002075002  
          6:CLOSE(DES1,LOCK);                                           0000633102076002

       END;                                                             0000633702082002  
    85 : CLOSE(ERRORES1,LOCK);                                          0000633802085002  
    90 : CLOSE(SDOS[0],LOCK);                                           0000633902098010  
    91 : CLOSE(SDOS[1],LOCK);                                           0000634002098012

    98 : CLOSE(SDOS[8],LOCK);                                           0000634702098082  
    99 : CLOSE(SDOS[9],LOCK);                                           0000634802098092  
%  222 : BEGIN                                                          0000634902099002  
%           ACT_MONITOR;                                                0000635002099052

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                               0000636902105002  
 PROCEDURE CONTROLVERS; % CONTROL DE VERSIONES                          0000637002106002  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                               0000637102107002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000637202108002  
    EBCDIC ARRAY CONSULTAS[0:11],LIBRERIA [0:71];                       0000637302109002  
    REPLACE CONSULTAS BY "S080BD01CON ";                                0000637402112002  
                                                                        0000637502112102

    PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6] ;                                             0000655302253117  
                                                                        0000655402253119  
    REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX BY 001059200201 FOR 12 DIGITS ;             0000655502253122  
    REPLACE PTR_EBC:PTR_EBC BY "        ",                              0000655602253127  
                                POINTER(NOM_LIB)+31 FOR 4,              0000655702253132  
%FLH                            SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                  0000655802253134  
                               "000000000001",                          0000655902253137  
                               "            ",                          0000656002253142  
                               "            ",                          0000656102253147  
                               "            ",                          0000656202253152  
                               "            " ;                         0000656302253157

                   "@" FOR 14,                                          0000656802253174

       ELSE                                                             0000661302253340

             BEGIN                                                      0000661502253347

    PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[156];                                           0000663502253437

                                   0000000 FOR 6 DIGITS,                0000663702253447  
                                   00      FOR 2 DIGITS ;               0000663802253472  
                                                                        0000663902253474  
    SIST      := 151;                                                   0000664002253477  
    RESULTADO := 0 ;                                                    0000664102253482  
    PROG      := 002 ;                                                  0000664202253487  
    BOOL_CAT  := FALSE ;                                                0000664302253492  
    REPLACE PAR_SAL[0] BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(PAR_SAL) ;                    0000664402253497  
                                                                        0000664502253499

       BEGIN                                                            0000664702253507  
          RESULTADO := L710_CONSUL_DETALLE(SIST,PROG,PAR_ENT,PAR_SAL) ; 0000664802253512

          PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[162] ;                                    0000665402253532  
          PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6] ;                                       0000665502253537  
          FECINI  := IDFSALFECONS(REG) ;                                0000665602253542  
          NIVINI  := IDFSALNIVINI(REG) ;                                0000665702253547

                                                                        0000666002253556

                INDS250 := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,12) ;                       0000666502253577

                   CVETIPMOV  := CVETRAN !! TIPMOV ;                    0000667602253632  
                   CATCVETRAN := DECIMAL(CVETIPMOV) ;                   0000667702253637

                END                                                     0000667902253647  
             ELSE                                                       0000668002253648  
                IF FORMATO = 4 THEN                                     0000668102253649  
                   BEGIN                                                0000668202253650  
                      NAT        := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,4) ;               0000668302253651  
                      INDS250    := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,5) ;               0000668402253652  
                      ICVETRAN   := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,1) ;               0000668502253653  
                      CLAVETR    := ICVETRAN            ;               0000668602253654  
                      ITIPMOV    := 1                   ;               0000668702253655  
                   END                                                  0000668802253660  
                ELSE                                                    0000668902253661  
                   BEGIN                                                0000669002253665  
                      CLAVETR := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,1) ;                  0000669102253666  
                      NAT     := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,11) ;                 0000669202253667  
                      INDS250 := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,12) ;                 0000669302253672  
                   END;                                                 0000669402253677  
                                                                        0000669502253679  
          PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6] ;                                       0000669602253682  
          REPLACE PTR_EBC:PTR_EBC BY DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,1) FOR 12,         0000669702253687  
                                     DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,2) FOR 12,         0000669802253692  
                                     DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,3) FOR 12,         0000669902253697  
                                     DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,4) FOR 12,         0000670002253702  
                                     DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,5) FOR 12,         0000670102253707  
                                     DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,6) FOR 12 ;        0000670202253712  
                                                                        0000670302253714  
          IF RESULTADO > 1 AND NOT BOOL_CAT THEN                        0000670402253717  
             BEGIN                                                      0000670502253722  
                REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",                0000670602253727  
                                  SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                0000670702253729  
                                  ", CATALOGO S080 VACIO, ",            0000670802253732  
                             " NO EXISTEN CLAVES DE TRANSACCION PARA S",0000670902253737

             END ;                                                      0000671202253752  
                                                                        0000671302253754  
          IF RESULTADO = 0 THEN RESULTADO := 3 ;                        0000671402253757  
                                                                        0000671502253759  
          BOOL_CAT := TRUE;                                             0000671602253762  
                                                                        0000671702253764  
          IF NAT = 1 OR NAT = 2 THEN                                    0000671802253767  
             IF INDS250 = 2 THEN                                        0000671902253772  
                REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 3 FOR 1 DIGITS          0000672002253777  
             ELSE                                                       0000672102253782  
                REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 1 FOR 1 DIGITS          0000672202253787  
          ELSE                                                          0000672302253792  
             IF INDS250 = 2 THEN                                        0000672402253797  
                REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 2   FOR 1 DIGITS ;      0000672502253802  
                                                                        0000672602253807  
          REPLACE ADFCVECOL  (CLAVETR) BY NAT FOR 1 DIGITS ;            0000672702253812  
                                                                        0000672802253817  
          CARGA_CATCT[ICVETRAN,(ITIPMOV-1)] := 1;                       0000672902253822  
       END ;                                                            0000673002253827  
                                                                        0000673102253829  
 END OF CARGACVES ;                                                     0000673202253832

    %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                     0000674602271002  
    PROCEDURE ARMALOG(LOGN,NIVLOG,FECDIA,NIVBASE);                      0000674702272002

    VALUE LOGN,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                                          0000674902274002

    BEGIN                                                               0000675102276002

                   NIV_SUP:=LOGS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                      0000678702313002  
                END                                                     0000678802314002  
             ELSE                                                       0000678902315002

                   IF HFUNCION = 99   THEN                              0000679902325002  
                      NIV_SUP := NUM_REG                                0000680002326002

             WHILE HFUNCION NEQ 99 DO                                   0000681102337002

                   END;                                                 0000681902360002  
           END                                                          0000682002361002  
       ELSE                                                             0000682102362002  
          IF NIVBASE > 0 THEN                                           0000682202363002  
             BEGIN                                                      0000682302364002

       ARMADES(K,NIVDES_A[K],ISISDFECARM(ACONTROLE1,K),NIVSDO_A[K]);    0000712902621002  
       NIVEL_K [K]:= ISISDNIVBDM(ACONTROLE1,K);                         0000713002622002  
%      STATUS_K[K]:= ISISDSTAARM(ACONTROLE1,K);                         0000713102623002  
    END;                                                                0000713202624002  
    IF NIVLOG_A[K] > NIVEL_K[K] AND B_INICIO AND                        0000713302625002  
       FEC_DIA [K]              =   FEC_CON  THEN                       0000713402626002  
%      STATUS_K[K]              =   0        THEN                       0000713502627002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000713602628002  
       NUMDIAC1:=K;                                                     0000713702629002  
       VERSION_P015;                                                    0000713802630002  
       REPLACE HHMMSS BY TIME(9) FOR 6   ;                              0000713902630102  
       HORA := INTEGER(POINTER(HHMMSS),6);                              0000714002630202  
       IF HORA < 200000 THEN VERSION_P016;                              0000714102631002  
       WAIT((3));                                                       0000714202641002  
       EXTERNO_P015;                                                    0000714302642002  
       IF HORA < 200000 THEN EXTERNO_P016;                              0000714402643752  
       NUMDIAC1:=0;                                                     0000714502643802  
    END;                                                                0000714602644002  
 END;                                                                   0000714702646002  
                                                                        0000714802650002  
 REPLACE NOM_ARCERR BY ARC_ERR FOR 34,                                  0000714902651002  
                       FEC_CON FOR 6 DIGITS,                            0000715002652002  
                       " ON "              ,                            0000715102657002  
                       PK_ERR  FOR 17,"."  ;                            0000715202658002  
                                                                        0000715302659002  
 REPLACE ERRORES1.TITLE BY NOM_ARCERR;                                  0000715402660002  
 IF ERRORES1.RESIDENT THEN                                              0000715502660102  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000715602660112  
    CLOSE(ERRORES1,LOCK);                                               0000715702660114  
    ERRORES1.MYUSE := 3;                                                0000715802660116  
    READ(ERRORES1[0],90,ELOG);                                          0000715902660122  
    FECHA_ERROR := INTEGER(POINTER(ELOG) + 2,8);                        0000716002660132  
    WHILE FEC_CON NEQ FECHA_ERROR DO                                    0000716102660142  
    BEGIN                                                               0000716202660152  
       REPLACE MSGLJ       BY HDRMSG,"I>04",                            0000716302660162  
                                     " ERROR EN FECHA HEADER ARCH ",    0000716402660172  
                                     NOM_ARCERR FOR 34,                 0000716502660182  
                                     " FECHA HD = ",                    0000716602660192  
                                     FECHA_ERROR FOR 8 DIGITS,          0000716702660202  
                                     " FECHA CORRECTA ",                0000716802660212  
                                     FEC_CON FOR 8 DIGITS;              0000716902660222  
       LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                 0000717002660232  
       ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                                  0000717102660242  
       CLOSE(ERRORES1,LOCK);                                            0000717202660243  
       READ(ERRORES1[0],90,ELOG);                                       0000717302660244  
       FECHA_ERROR := INTEGER(POINTER(ELOG) + 2,8);                     0000717402660246  
    END;                                                                0000717502660252  
    NIVERROR1 := ERRORES1.LASTRECORD;                                   0000717602660262  
    NIVERROR1 := * + 1;                                                 0000717702660267  
 END                                                                    0000717802660292  
 ELSE                                                                   0000717902660302  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000718002660322  
    ERRORES1.MYUSE := 1;                                                0000718102660323  
    REPLACE ELOG BY "HD",                                               0000718202660324  
                    FEC_CON FOR 8 DIGITS;                               0000718302660326  
    WRITE(ERRORES1[0],90,ELOG);                                         0000718402660328  
    NIVERROR1 := 1;                                                     0000718502660342  
    CLOSE(ERRORES1,LOCK);                                               0000718602660392  
    ERRORES1.MYUSE := 3;                                                0000718702660442  
 END;                                                                   0000718802660502  
 END LEVANTA;                                                           0000718902661002

    PROCEDURE COMMANDPOST;                                              0000719902662082  
       BEGIN                                                            0000720002662092  
          INTEGER LONGITUD, RESIDUO;                                    0000720102662102  
          POINTER P_LEN;                                                0000720202662107  
                                                                        0000720302662109  
          EXCEPTION PROCEDURE LIBERA;                                   0000720402662112  
             BEGIN                                                      0000720502662122  
                IF LOCKSTATUS (LK_COMANDPOST).[47:24] = PROCESSID THEN  0000720602662132  
                   UNLOCK(LK_COMANDPOST);                               0000720702662142  
             END;                                                       0000720802662152  
                                                                        0000720902662157  
          LONGITUD := SIZE(E_PARAM);                                    0000721002662162

          SCAN P_LEN FOR RESIDUO:LONGITUD UNTIL = 48"00";               0000721302662169

                                      "/PR",PROCE FOR 2 DIGITS," ";     0000722002662227  
                                                                        0000722102662229  
          IF LONGITUD < 62 THEN                                         0000722202662232  
             REPLACE DF_CPOST_TXT BY "[",                               0000722302662242  
                                     E_PARAM FOR LONGITUD,              0000722402662244  
                                     "] ",                              0000722502662246  
                                     "[] [] []"                         0000722602662252  
          ELSE                                                          0000722702662262  
             IF LONGITUD < 124 THEN                                     0000722802662272  
                BEGIN                                                   0000722902662282  
                   REPLACE DF_CPOST_TXT BY "[",                         0000723002662292  
                                           E_PARAM FOR 62,              0000723102662294  
                                           "] ",                        0000723202662296  
                                           "[",                         0000723302662302  
                                           E_PARAM[62] FOR LONGITUD-62, 0000723402662307  
                                           "] ",                        0000723502662312  
                                           "[] []";                     0000723602662317  
                END                                                     0000723702662322  
             ELSE                                                       0000723802662332  
                IF LONGITUD < 186 THEN                                  0000723902662342  
                   BEGIN                                                0000724002662352  
                      REPLACE DF_CPOST_TXT BY "[",                      0000724102662362  
                                              E_PARAM FOR 62,           0000724202662364  
                                              "] ",                     0000724302662366  
                                              "[",                      0000724402662372  
                                              E_PARAM[ 62] FOR 62,      0000724502662377  
                                              "] [",                    0000724602662382  
                                          E_PARAM[124] FOR LONGITUD-124,0000724702662392  
                                              "] []";                   0000724802662402  
                   END;                                                 0000724902662412  
                                                                        0000725002662414  
          REPLACE MSGLJ BY POINTER(A_CPOST) FOR (SIZE(A_CPOST)*6);      0000725102662416  
         DF_DISPLAY("E>050110<",POINTER(A_CPOST) FOR (SIZE(A_CPOST)*6));0000725202662422  
                                                                        0000725302662424  
          IF LLAMO_A_CPOST THEN                                         0000725402662427  
             BEGIN                                                      0000725502662430  
                RSENDAUTO(A_CPOST);                                     0000725602662432  
                DELINKLIBRARY(LIB_OUTBOARD);                            0000725702662433  
                REPLACE EA_I_CPST[0] BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(EA_I_CPST);     0000725802662434  
             END;                                                       0000725902662438  
                                                                        0000726002662440  
          UNLOCK(LK_COMANDPOST);                                        0000726102662442  
       END OF COMMANDPOST;                                              0000726202662452  
                                                                        0000726302662462

%***************                                                        0000729802690002  
 PROCEDURE BAJA;                                                        0000729902691002  
%***************                                                        0000730002692002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000730102693002  
   INTEGER I,J, RESULT_L001, ESTADO, MEZCLA;                            0000730202694002  
                                                                        0000730302695002  
   BOOLEAN BOOL_FIN;                                                    0000730402696002  
   LABEL REGRESA;                                                       0000730502697002  
                                                                        0000730602698002  
   CLOSE (ERRORES1,LOCK);                                               0000730702700002  
   I:= -1;                                                              0000730802701002  
   WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                                0000730902702002  
         CIERRALOG(I);                                                  0000731002704002  
   REGRESA:                                                             0000731102707002  
   BOOL_FIN:= TRUE;                                                     0000731202708002  
   I:= -1;                                                              0000731302709002  
   WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                                0000731402710002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000731502711002  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P015(I) > 0 THEN                                    0000731602712002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000731702713002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000731802718002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P015(I) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",         0000731902719002  
                           48"00";                                      0000732002720002  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000732102721002  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000732202722002  
      END;                                                              0000732302723002  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P016(I) > 0 THEN                                    0000732402723012  
      BEGIN                                                             0000732502723022  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000732602723032  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P016(I) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",         0000732702723042  
                           48"00";                                      0000732802723052  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000732902723062  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000733002723072  
      END;                                                              0000733102723082  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P025 > 0 THEN                                       0000733202724002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000733302725002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000733402730002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P025 FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",            0000733502731002  
                           48"00";                                      0000733602732002  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000733702733002  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000733802734002  
      END;                                                              0000733902735002  
   END;                                                                 0000734002736002  
   BOOL_FIN := FALSE;                                                   0000734102736052  
   I:= 0            ;                                                   0000734202736072  
   ESTADO := STATUS_MIX_P025    ;                                       0000734302736102  
   MEZCLA := NUM_MIX_P025       ;                                       0000734402736152  
   VALIDA_STATUS(ESTADO, MEZCLA);                                       0000734502736202  
   WHILE (NOT BOOL_FIN) AND (I LEQ 9) DO                                0000734602736252  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P015(I) > 0 OR                                      0000734702736302  
         STATUS_MIX_P016(I) > 0 OR                                      0000734802736502  
         STATUS_MIX_P025    > 0 THEN                                    0000734902736552  
      BEGIN                                                             0000735002736602  
         BOOL_FIN := TRUE;                                              0000735102736652  
         ESTADO := STATUS_MIX_P015(I) ;                                 0000735202736702  
         MEZCLA := NUM_MIX_P015(I)    ;                                 0000735302736752  
         VALIDA_STATUS(ESTADO, MEZCLA);                                 0000735402736802  
         ESTADO := STATUS_MIX_P016(I) ;                                 0000735502736852  
         MEZCLA := NUM_MIX_P016(I)    ;                                 0000735602736902  
         VALIDA_STATUS(ESTADO, MEZCLA);                                 0000735702736952  
      END                                                               0000735802736972  
      ELSE                                                              0000735902737002  
         I := * + 1;                                                    0000736002738002  
   IF BOOL_FIN THEN GO REGRESA;                                         0000736102739002  
   REPLACE ACONTROLE2 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROLE2);                   0000736202740002  
   REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROLE2) BY    22 FOR 2 DIGITS,              0000736302741002  
                                    SIST_LIB FOR 4 DIGITS,              0000736402742002  
                                    0        FOR 8 DIGITS,              0000736502742012  
                                    2        FOR 4 DIGITS;              0000736602742022  
   RESULT_L001:= B05PROCESOS(ACONTROLE2);                               0000736702742032  
 END OF BAJA;                                                           0000736802743002  
%============================================================           0000736902743022  
%       INICIA PROCEDIMIENTO DE CONTROL                     =           0000737002743042  
%============================================================           0000737102743062  
 PROCEDURE PROC_CONTROL;                                                0000737202743082  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000737302743102  
   INTEGER EVENTO, NUM_HI;                                              0000737402743122  
   EBCDIC ARRAY DAT_CONTROL[0:11];                                      0000737502743142  
   NUM_HI := 0;                                                         0000737602743152  
   WHILE NUM_HI NEQ 4 DO                                                0000737702743162  
   BEGIN                                                                0000737802743182  
      EVENTO := WAITANDRESET((2), MYSELF.EXCEPTIONEVENT);               0000737902743202  
      CASE EVENTO OF                                                    0000738002743222  
      BEGIN                                                             0000738102743242  
         1    : EVENTO := 0;                                            0000738202743262  
         2    : BEGIN                                                   0000738302743282  
                  NUM_HI := MYSELF.TASKVALUE;                           0000738402743302  
                  REPLACE DAT_CONTROL[0] BY "0" FOR 12;                 0000738502743322  
                  REPLACE DAT_CONTROL[0] BY NUM_HI FOR 4 DIGITS;        0000738602743342  
                  CONTROLES(DAT_CONTROL);                               0000738702743352  
                  RESET(MYSELF.EXCEPTIONEVENT);                         0000738802743354  
                  MYSELF.TASKVALUE := 0;                                0000738902743356  
               END;                                                     0000739002743362  
         ELSE :    ;                                                    0000739102743382  
      END ;                                                             0000739202743402  
   END;                                                                 0000739302743422  
   WHILE MYSELF.LIBRARYUSERS > 0 DO                                     0000739402743522  
         EVENTO := WAITANDRESET((2), MYSELF.EXCEPTIONEVENT);            0000739502743622  
   MYSELF.STATUS := VALUE(GOINGAWAY);                                   0000739602743722  
 END;                                                                   0000739702744002  
                                                                        0000739802745002

     RES_LOCK := UNLOCK(CAMBIA_FECHA);                                  0000740802754102
